The [RW lifecycle report](rw_lifecycle_report.md) resolves IO RW allocation/free core interfaces, `site.lck_rw_old_t` metadata, 16-byte initialization and linked free-validation prefixes. It retains a bounded eleven-site modifier census, one excluded internal helper, eight unresolved candidates and 64 shared-store state candidates. 284 fresh cases, four proofs and two SDK signatures pass. Cached-cell publication, runtime group/zone initialization and native lock lifetime remain unknown; see RL1-RL9.

The [sample-lock report](sample_lock_report.md) resolves conditional mutex/RW acquisition and release adapters surrounding the coordinator in both declared contexts. The pre-coordinator helper's post-initializer fixture label is refined to an acquisition-call context. Source-written preparer errors feed 24 reconstructed caller continuations before acquisition; caller status snapshots remain separate from void lock returns. 140 fresh cases, nine proofs, ten exhaustive banks and four SDK signatures pass. Native lock identity/lifetime, full carrier provenance and serialization remain unknown; see SL1-SL9.

The [carrier-control report](carrier_control_report.md) connects both builder preparer calls to `0xfffffe000a621118`, recovers a separate partial 64-byte record codec, and establishes a complete 95-byte reset footprint within a 104-byte accessed extent. Local producers assign control pairs `0/1` and `1/1`; 192 fresh cases, four proofs and six exhaustive banks pass. The complete first-byte domain at the coordinator/provider boundary, intervening pointer identity and native behavior remain unknown. Eight literal SP pivots qualify the initial-frame translations; see CC1-CC8.

The [metadata and AES-transfer report](sample_transfer_report.md) connects raw size words to buffered/direct submission, distinct counters and typed IV/key-data provider arguments. 201 fresh prefixes, 103 original-wrapper compositions and eight proofs pass. Enum-argument equality across paths requires a 0/1 first-byte domain that is not yet established. All crypto/provider operations remain stopped; post-submission output, native ownership and original input reachability remain unknown. See ST1-ST8.

The [state construction and workspace report](sample_state_report.md) recovers packed carrier spans, a complete 360-byte fill helper, a kernel copy-alias binding, raw state-control partitions and an 8192-byte workspace lifecycle. 7148 fresh cases, six proofs and source/model event reconstruction pass. Ten nonempty cases stop before a four-byte sample-size read; business control names, native lifetime and downstream processing remain unknown. See SS1-SS8.

The [descriptor caller and builder report](sample_caller_report.md) connects the sample-size setup to hardware initialization and samples setup, recovers a 96-byte internal record and two task-carrier lineages, and distinguishes stored status from incidental release-register values. 113 caller cases, 48 builder cases, 12 proofs and 3840 builder-written bytes pass fresh verification. Full frame predecessors, ultimate task identity and native lifetime remain unknown; see SR1-SR8.

The [media descriptor lifecycle report](sample_descriptor_report.md) recovers the common factory/class for four descriptor globals, task-dependent type bits, exact copy-method tail bindings, and the full64 samples-to-AES bridge. Source create helpers overwrite their global; release/complete helpers have no own global reset. Caller coordination and native lifetime remain unproved. 276 fresh cases, four proofs and two exhaustive adapter banks are checked. See M1-M8.

The [dispatch-context report](kext_aes_dispatch_report.md) distinguishes constant byte-bank target selection from payload-dependent record dispatch. Eighteen source pointer lineages and a 33-record literal frame-store census support declared contexts; actual reaching definitions and alias/callee preservation remain unknown. Fresh checks cover 11264 original dispatch slices, 32 classifier cases, and six local proofs, including a partial cleanup record codec. See R1-R8.

The [AES handoff report](kext_aes_handoff_report.md) connects kext wrapper arguments to a partial IOCryptoAcceleratorFamily command carrier, queued sleep/wakeup and callback ordering. It resolves one obfuscated cleanup caller through exhaustive banks, separates five remaining candidates, and distinguishes linkedit metadata matches from runtime pointer candidates. Eighty-five fresh wrapper cases verify call order and stack epochs; native provider behavior and eventual cleanup reachability remain unknown. See H1-H9.

The [buffer descriptor lifecycle report](kext_buffer_report.md) connects original factory/class construction, `prepare(0)`, the three-path `getBytesNoCopy()` accessor and ordered cleanup. Options `0x13` match installed SDK bidirectional/physically contiguous flags. Cleanup clears readiness and requests completion/release under explicit pointer guards. Fresh getter and cleanup runs verify data reads, call order and global writes; valid native lifecycle states, serialization and hardware transfer remain unknown. See D1-D8.

The [cold initialization analysis](kext_cold_init_report.md) follows `_oh70aLVr` through a declared 104-byte `site.FPAESParameters` allocation, 31 source-initialized bytes and IOAESAccelerator discovery. The source passes `{0,1}` to the matching kernel waiter, requesting 1 ns. Eight fresh cases verify the partial heap ABI and provider-absent cleanup; two complete modeled failures return `0xffff5bea` with close flag and registry pointer still zero. The nonnull branch statically requests an 81920-byte buffer; runtime alignment, provider success and complete initialization remain unknown. See A1-A8.

The [kext close-wrapper record analysis](kext_close_report.md) recovers low32 token marshalling,an address-keyed `0xc18` dispatch record and a byte output in `_oh70aLVr`. Four declared flag1/null-global-pointer cases reject before reading the token field and explicitly return `0xffff5b36`;a flag0 case stops at typed allocation. Five fresh prefixes,five scoped proofs,four byte-bank identities and256 fresh daemon IDB bytes are checked. Real initialization,successful finalization,extra-tag association and native port lifetime remain unknown;see K1-K8.

The [RPC700 namespace and receiver-candidate analysis](rpc700_namespace_report.md) verifies the matching kernel getter's1..36 index domain and conditional null/status0 result. The installed SDK names slot34 `HOST_MANAGEDAPPDISTD_PORT`;that label does not identify a receiver. Two managed-app arm64e slices,5174 imports,50 rejected common-MIG header patterns and298 framework GOT records provide bounded negative evidence. RPC700 receiver,alternate-mode reachability and native port identity remain unknown;see N1-N8.

The [RPC502 cleanup-argument analysis](rpc502_cleanup_report.md) identifies a shared helper at `0x100444704`:the supplemental caller stores mode0 for close-only;the primary caller stores mode1 for unmap-then-close. Five local proofs establish the address-keyed codec and alignment domain. Twenty-four original/fresh helper fixtures verify full64-bit extent forwarding and descriptor=-1 after modeled close failure. Native lifetime,ownership transfer and actual IPC remain unknown;see C1-C8.

The [RPC502 ownership and error-delivery analysis](rpc502_ownership_report.md) reconciles all29 resource calls per complete worker case. Both declared descriptors close and the primary mapping unmaps;the supplemental mapping remains live at return. Eight original callback fixtures and eight fresh replays compose the observed error into a36-byte ID602 reply,with incoming-message destruction requested after clearing its reply-right field. Native resource effects,complete ownership transfer and successful processing remain unknown. See assumptions O1-O8.

The preceding exported-block increment recovers post-export byte contracts:header,FF region,separator,original SHA-1/SHA-256 DigestInfo literals,digest-tail comparison and local mismatch/status folds. Two complete fresh workers cover six windows,29969824 retained/fresh source visits each,30042711 assertions,12 source spans,1541 pointer records and seven local proofs. Four certificate comparisons match;two file comparisons fail and contain multiple mismatched regions. No complete acceptance language,new native Mach/IOKit emission or accepted signed operation is established. See [exported-block contracts](rpc502_padding_report.md),B1-B8. All published predecessors remain frozen;the broad interface goal remains active and incomplete.

## Immediate source leads

The ADID path now includes complete command selection and a full original-callback chain through a144-byte Avp100 request,returned-count control and a source-built320-byte second request. Immediate leads are the matching Avp type0/method100 implementation;accepted response schemas and original successful consumers for both sizes;the opaque16-/32-byte producers and deeper transfers;compact semantics of the12-/1-byte repeated state computation;complete size64/count32/capacity/alias/ownership and cache/retry/close behavior;actual CoreADI client producers/all fourteen command schemas;valid constructor inner blob and normal collection publication/persistence;native image/credential/libxpc/IOKit/Mach endpoint and right identity. Finite time/random controls affect only first-packet bytes94..97;full dependence/meaning remains unknown. See [Avp packets and count control](adid_avp_packet_report.md),[147690 checks and320-byte packets](triage_evidence/adid_avp_packet_checks.json),[command dispatch/request-to-Avp](adid_command_dispatch_report.md),[record lifecycle](adid_record_lifecycle_report.md),[collection lookup/update](adid_context_lookup_report.md),[protected dispatch](adid_driver_dispatch_report.md),[IOKit callers](adid_iokit_caller_report.md),[IOKit boundary](adid_iokit_report.md) and [CoreFP client paths](corefp_client_paths_report.md). The arcade path has private completed status0 replies for decision0 and decision1,with AppStore repair assembly on the latter. Broker capability,local alternate-notification consumption,protected AMS initialization and CoreFP version/record/Mach-builder boundaries are recovered within stated assumptions. Further leads are actual CoreFP/daemon pairing and the metadata method-ID difference,remaining acquired-name/flags consumers,reply parsers and OOL/right lifetime;mode7/certificate protocol and remaining downstream IOKit/XPC/bootstrap calls;plain/trusted event producer provenance;family-content-deletion recipients;appstored recent-bag/authorizeMachine protocol;successful protected import/allocation/persistence;NSXPC serialization/rights;effective CMS/cache pairing and decoder-failure transitions;complete signer/receipt/trust semantics;protected repair routine0x1003afe84;daemon event callback0x100460008;policy table+0x1c8 and kernel pending-event writers. Cached-object history,startup gates,OOL/right ownership and voucher initialization remain open.

1. The request word at FP-0xe0 -> record+0x24 is now traced to `_fcHfFIGhsx` argument5; all three direct provider callers pass1. Continue with alternate/tag0x85150332 callers and full transition/alias proofs. Origin is a separate word+0x20, and UserClient virtual mode remains a separate interface.
2. Extend the newly separated scratch/argument identities through normal context publication and `_oh70aLVr` close. The release at `0xfffffe000a615f2c` is established on a guarded post-RPC rejection path; older candidate `0xfffffe000a60ced8` still lacks bank/table/frame proof. Complete allocation-capacity arithmetic around `0xfffffe000a60b518` and fill extent at `...b71c` before interpreting oversized-name behavior.
3. Callback `0x100425f78` routes all five families; reply emission0x100035184 has an available libSystem -> mach_msg -> Mach2 -> SVC0x180547c1c -> original kernel Mach table[-47] -> handler0xfffffe000bcdd73c path. Name lookup0xfffffe000bcd2cc0 and receive wrapper0xfffffe000bcc8cf4 now establish post-lock table/object revalidation, generation/rights and lock/retain contracts; receive-results0xfffffe000bcdce04 releases the receive-object reference. Continue through namespace writers, CF cached-object/service registration history, actual voucher hooks, complete message-body disposal and real endpoint matching. User thread+0x450 replacement clears/releases an old binding, while kernel thread+0x3f0 unbind reaches destruction; destination object and turnstile link holds are separate from both bindings and message rights. Invalid receive arguments can follow an attempted send; voucher tables and task/space policy alter packets. RAX resumptions, native library retries and BSD restart remain distinct. Attest producer/modules and preserve exact-image/reference differences.
4. Continue RPC502 at CoreKE `0x100de9c6c`, global0x1010b9630 and split `0x100787b9c`; recover complete CoreKE inputs/results, global writers/reset and native framing. Follow the newly recovered mmap at `0x100c85e40`, malloc at `0x10095b5c8`, mapping-result publication at `0x100dacb58` and first-word reader `0x1009b6eac` into complete size/count guards, valid parser schema and successful reply ownership. Continue rejected-pattern cleanup beyond free `0x1008ec710` to unmap/close. Resolve early fstat/mapping-failure descriptor aliases; distinguish later cleanup's descriptor invalidation from actual resource retirement. The reached QoS exit at `0x1005eeb98` requests saved class/relative0; complete all exits and setter-failure behavior. Preserve unknown data and record-reuse epochs. The VMM branch remains a local loader decision; independently expand RPC700/800, BootPolicy/SEP, service/audit and scheduling coverage.
5. Propagate the exact collection symbol map into semantic recovery of other indirect kernel calls, without modifying the original IDBs or replacing scoped native evidence with automatic model diagnostics.
6. Follow the text-pager callback consumers below IOTextEncryptionFamily, the 32-bit token lookup and `_oh70aLVr` finalization, plus AppleAVD's `_AVDDecryptParams` boundary. Inspect origin-sensitive callers before interpreting the cache's omission of origin. Preserve source-correlated kernel names as interpretations rather than exact symbols.
7. Continue below the recovered kernel RPC and reply-link contracts: full turnstile/workloop/knote state and external callout behavior, namespace allocation/growth/reclamation, destination copyout0xfffffe000bcca164 and destruction0xfffffe000bccdbb4. Link0xfffffe000bccef30, prepare0xfffffe000bcced98, complete0xfffffe000bcceadc and adjust0xfffffe000bcce5b8 now have original ranges and selected reference/state proofs. Match exact source before promoting stripped helper names to original symbols. Preserve caller send reference, message COPY_SEND, receive-object retain, destination link hold, turnstile hold and user/kernel thread bindings as separate scopes.
